# Receipt audit, release 266 (2026-10-30)

The owner, 2026-10-30: "pick apart and cut down this testing suitte SIGNIFICANTLY and make super strenuous suites like balance and other testing to be not for every deploy annd manually recommended annd triggered by ME".

Every receipt registered at ccb2041c41 (2,122) has one decision; this lane adds one, `tools/selftest-tiers.selftest.mjs` (default tier: the tiers, their callers, lane mode and the recommender, with negative controls). The tiers live in `tools/selftest-tiers.mjs`; this page is generated from it (`node tools/test-audit.mjs --write`, never by hand): `tools/selftest-tiers.selftest.mjs` fails when its counts or decisions differ from the tiers (run times, which differ by machine, are not compared).

- **KEEP**: the default tier. `npm test`, the release gate (`tools/gate.mjs`) and tank:release:check's embedded suite run it; lane mode (`npm run test:lane`) runs the part a diff reaches.
- **MANUAL**: a named suite, run only by `npm run test:heavy -- <suite>`. `npm run test:recommend` says which suites a diff warrants and why; Main relays it and the owner triggers the run. `tanks` is id-scoped: lane mode and tank:release:check run the receipts naming or reading their ids.
- **MERGE**: folded into a named receipt with no coverage lost.
- **RETIRE**: removed with `git rm` (history keeps it), with the reason and where its coverage lives.

Run times: `tools/selftest-durations.json`, else this machine's latest observation (265 receipts registered since the snapshot; observed under load 150-400, so they read high). Wall time replays the runner's schedule on an idle machine with 8 workers.

|---|---:|---:|---:|

Default tier: 11.4 % of the whole registry's CPU; 15.8 % of the 916 CPU-min the snapshot gave for the 1,863 receipts it timed. Its wall: 0.3 min exclusive receipts, 17.7 min pool, 2.5 min browser receipts.

## Manual suites

| Suite | Receipts | CPU-min | What it covers |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `balance` | 3 | 1.1 | fleet round-robin duels on the authoritative battle path and the balance tables they hold; then `node tools/balance-matchups.mjs --check` |
| `pacing` | 13 | 93.9 | whole-match pacing (the 132 default lobbies in eight shards and their judge), placement and deployment on every map |
| `bots` | 8 | 40.4 | bot behaviour played out on the authority (every mode, objectives, levels, approaches) |
| `sims` | 7 | 5.0 | drone, missile, escort and counter-drone scenario runs |
| `perf` | 4 | 2.5 | host-time budgets (tick cost, soaks, heap plateaus, restore timing): they measure the machine as much as the code |
| `fleet` | 60 | 245.6 | fleet-wide vehicle sweeps: every playable tank built (the HIGH, LOW and default fleet passes, the lazy facade, floor clearance, vertex colours, national and fleet-wide fitting sweeps) and the whole-fleet anatomy check (tank:anatomy:check); then `node tools/gen-combat-anatomy.mjs --check`, `node tools/gen-vehicle-marking-seats.mjs --check`, `node tools/module-hit-probe.mjs`, `node tools/tank-assets-check.mjs --views armorSide,modulesSide,crewSide --skip-bore`, `node tools/spare-track-placement-check.mjs --ids=all --ledger=docs/geometry-gate/spare-track.json --check --quiet`, `node tools/interior-fill-exposure.mjs --all --check --jobs=4` |
| `tanks` | 418 | 292.0 | per-tank and per-family pins (id-scoped: lane mode and tank:release:check run the ones naming or reading the lane's ids) |
| `world` | 138 | 406.8 | world-wide map builds: scenery, structure collision, buildings, the wreck bake, the collision shards, civic areas, horizons and map content |
| `ui` | 6 | 8.6 | heavy interface paint and layout sweeps |
| `browser` | 4 | 24.7 | strenuous real-battle browser receipts (a GPU and the capture lock each) |

## Must-keep receipts (default tier, never manual)

Sim determinism and driver boundaries, the authority rules, armour/ballistics/damage on a representative sample, and the typecheck, build, budget, attribution, security, i18n and changed-only guards. `tools/selftest-tiers.selftest.mjs` fails if one leaves the default tier. The guards with their own commands (`npm run typecheck`, `npm run build`, `npm run check:bundle`, `check:frontend`, `attribution:check`) are not receipts and are unchanged.

- `tools/driver-boundaries.selftest.mjs`: the simulation drivers keep to their boundaries (sim determinism)
- `src/sim/driverStepParity.selftest.mjs`: every driver steps the simulation bit for bit alike (sim determinism)
- `server/convexOutlines.selftest.mjs`: convex armour parts hold their outline in the shell clip (must-keep: armour sample)
- `server/match/frameView.selftest.mjs`: a viewer frame from the authority's view: spotting filters before serialization (must-keep)
- `server/match/interestTiers.selftest.mjs`: per-viewer interest tiers (must-keep: what a client is sent)
- `server/match/matchActor.selftest.mjs`: the match actor (must-keep: authority)
- `src/mp/host/droneRecallWire.selftest.mjs`: the client only asks; the authority decides (must-keep)
- `src/mp/host/hostRuleset.selftest.mjs`: the room arrangement reaches the match (must-keep: authority)
- `src/mp/host/migrationState.selftest.mjs`: host migration seal (AES-GCM) and keyframe codec (must-keep: security)

## MERGE (0)

None. Folding receipts saves only process starts (about a second each) and would need a per-assertion coverage review; the CPU is in the receipts that build tanks and maps, which moved to manual suites instead.
